- **llmstxt**: Author a spec-compliant llms.txt (and, where it earns its place, the de-facto llms-full.txt) from a body of written work — a docs tree, a website, a repo, a portfolio, or a developer accomplishment/brag document. - **llo-uat**: Run user acceptance testing with onboarded LLOs: send instructions, track feedback, compile sign-off status. ([dimagi-internal/ace](https://github.com/dimagi-internal/ace/tree/HEAD/skills/llo-uat) · ★1)
- **llo-uat-eval**: Grade an llo-uat results compilation against PDD success metrics — coverage completeness, sign-off clarity, blocker resolution. ([dimagi-internal/ace](https://github.com/dimagi-internal/ace/tree/HEAD/skills/llo-uat-eval) · ★1)
- **llomics-clusters**: Build an llomics payload from clustering plus marker ranking (Scanpy rank_genes_groups, Seurat FindAllMarkers, or any one-vs-rest marker table): one state per cluster with size, QC facts compared to the dataset, top markers, a canonical marker panel, and trust checks. Use for single-cell or spatial cluster annotation, cluster QC triage, and doublet or low-quality cluster review. ([IOsinnii/llomics](https://github.com/IOsinnii/llomics/tree/HEAD/skills/llomics-clusters))
- **llomics-core**: The llomics payload contract: how to turn the output of a statistical or ML step in an omics pipeline (PCA, limma/DESeq2, GLM, lasso, PLS-DA, clustering and marker ranking) into a compact, self-describing JSON payload that an LLM or a typed-judgment model can reason over. Use when a pipeline step produces more rows than a person can read, when writing or reviewing the "summary for the model" step of a pipeline, and before using any other llomics-* skill. ([IOsinnii/llomics](https://github.com/IOsinnii/llomics/tree/HEAD/skills/llomics-core))
- **llomics-glm**: Build an llomics payload from a generalised linear model (R glm, statsmodels GLM or Logit, logistic, Poisson, negative binomial): one state per fitted model with coefficients on an interpretable scale, fit statistics, and diagnostics for convergence, separation, overdispersion, and collinearity. Use when a model has many terms or many models were fitted, and the question is which effects are real and whether the fit can be trusted. ([IOsinnii/llomics](https://github.com/IOsinnii/llomics/tree/HEAD/skills/llomics-glm))
- **llomics-judge**: How to consume an llomics payload: split each decision into measured facts (code) and judgments (model), ask typed questions of one unit at a time, and turn the answers into auto-accept or manual-review decisions with reason codes. Use when writing the annotation, triage, or interpretation step that follows a payload, with a typed-judgment model such as TypeSafe Jev, a structured-output LLM, or a reasoning agent. ([IOsinnii/llomics](https://github.com/IOsinnii/llomics/tree/HEAD/skills/llomics-judge))
- **llomics-lasso**: Build an llomics payload from a lasso or elastic-net model (glmnet, cv.glmnet, scikit-learn LassoCV, ElasticNetCV, LogisticRegressionCV with an L1 penalty): one state per model at the chosen penalty with cross-validated performance against a null model, the selected features with selection frequency, and their correlated stand-ins. Use when a sparse model picked a signature from thousands of features and the question is whether the signature is stable and what it means. ([IOsinnii/llomics](https://github.com/IOsinnii/llomics/tree/HEAD/skills/llomics-lasso))
- **llomics-limma**: Build an llomics payload from a feature-wise differential analysis (limma, limma-voom, and the same-shaped tables from DESeq2 and edgeR): one state per contrast with hit counts, top up and down features, a control panel, the shape of the p-value distribution, and trust checks. Use when a topTable or results table has thousands of rows and the question is what changed, how much, and whether the result can be believed. ([IOsinnii/llomics](https://github.com/IOsinnii/llomics/tree/HEAD/skills/llomics-limma))
- **llomics-pca**: Build an llomics payload from a PCA (prcomp, scikit-learn PCA, Scanpy sc.tl.pca): one state per component with variance explained, top positive and negative loadings, association with sample metadata, group positions, and outliers. Use when asking what each principal component represents, whether a component is biological or technical, or whether batch drives the main axes of variation. ([IOsinnii/llomics](https://github.com/IOsinnii/llomics/tree/HEAD/skills/llomics-pca))
- **llomics-plsda**: Build an llomics payload from PLS-DA, sPLS-DA, or OPLS-DA (mixOmics, ropls, scikit-learn PLSRegression on dummy-coded classes): a model state with cross-validated performance and a permutation test, and one state per component with high-VIP features, their direction, and a univariate cross-check. Use for metabolomics, proteomics, or lipidomics class discrimination when the question is which features separate the groups and whether the separation is real. ([IOsinnii/llomics](https://github.com/IOsinnii/llomics/tree/HEAD/skills/llomics-plsda))
- **llomics-synthesis**: Combine llomics payloads from several methods run on the same data (for example limma, lasso, PLS-DA, and PCA) into one evidence table per feature, and separate agreement that strengthens a finding from agreement that only repeats it. Use when the same dataset was analysed more than one way and the question is which findings hold across methods and which belong to one method. ([IOsinnii/llomics](https://github.com/IOsinnii/llomics/tree/HEAD/skills/llomics-synthesis))
- **lloydbriantech-ascent**: ASCENT engineering framework. Use to scaffold new projects, enhance existing ASCENT projects, migrate non-ASCENT projects to ASCENT standards, or answer questions about ASCENT's conventions, principles, or nine engineering roles (delivery-lead, architect, ui-ux-designer, developer, data-engineer, ai-engineer, tester, devops, cybersecurity). ([aibot88/sec_skill_store](https://github.com/aibot88/sec_skill_store/tree/HEAD/skills/claudskills/lloydbriantech-ascent) · ★6)
- **lloydbriantech-ascent**: Use when starting a new ASCENT project, enhancing or migrating a project to ASCENT standards, or asking about ASCENT's conventions, principles or nine engineering roles (delivery-lead, architect, ui-ux-designer, developer, data-engineer, ai-engineer, tester, devops, cybersecurity). Inside a scaffolded project, day-to-day tasks such as ADRs, audits and status belong to the project's ascent-* skills. - **llp-adopt**: Bring LLP to a repository or package and keep installed skills current. Scaffold, retrofit (survey + maintainer interview + provenance-tagged drafts), install from a tagged release, update without clobbering forks. Use when the user runs /llp-adopt, asks to set up LLP in a repo or package, or update the LLP skills. ([ccheever/llp](https://github.com/ccheever/llp/tree/HEAD/skills/llp-adopt) · ★93)

- **llp-create**: Create a new LLP document in this repo with the next available number, the correct filename convention (NNNN-slug.type.md), and a scaffolded metadata header ready to fill in. ([hyparam/hypaware](https://github.com/hyparam/hypaware/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/llp-create) · ★17)
- **llp-grill**: Grilling session that stress-tests a plan against the project's LLP corpus (Specs, Decisions, Principles) and Systems vocabulary, then captures crystallised decisions inline by updating, creating, superseding, or tombstoning LLP documents and flagging where code will need @ref annotations. - **llp-init-retrofit**: Add LLP to an existing repository by surveying the codebase and docs, drafting the root LLP, and proposing a phased adoption and annotation plan. ([hyparam/hypaware](https://github.com/hyparam/hypaware/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/llp-init-retrofit) · ★17)
- **llp-list**: List the LLP documents in this repository, grouped by status or filtered by type, system, or status. - **llp-maintain**: Keep an LLP corpus in sync with its code. Detects code/doc drift, validates @ref annotations interactively, checks provenance tags, and proposes reconciliations, ref repairs, status changes, and overlay changes. Modes — pre-pr, audit, reconcile, retire-proposal, curate (the promote → archive → realign pass). Proposes; never applies. - **llp-orient**: Before editing a subsystem, file, or starting a task, assemble the governing LLP context — read the root LLP, follow the @ref annotations on the code in scope, and find the LLPs covering the relevant systems. - **llp-orient**: Use before editing code or starting a task in a repo with an llp/ folder: gathers the root LLP and the LLPs the code's @refs point to, and returns the constraints the change must respect. ([peterpme/skills](https://github.com/peterpme/skills/tree/HEAD/llp-orient) · ★29)
- **llp-review**: Run the LLP 0005 review loop for an LLP document — orchestrate reviews from other model families, write one provenance-tracked artifact per review actually received under llp/reviews/, and never fabricate a review or accept on the author's behalf. - **llp-super-refine**: Author-invoked dual-family refine loop for one or more Draft/Review LLPs — each round, two fresh mutually-blind reviews from different model families, orchestrator revision, verdict-lined artifacts — until both families mark the same revision READY, a delta round finds no MATERIAL concern in pre-existing text across families, or a bounded stop rule escalates to the author. Use when the user invokes /llp-super-refine or asks to refine an LLP until independent models agree it is ready. - **llp-super-refine**: Use when Peter types /llp-super-refine or wants a Draft or Review LLP refined until two model families independently mark the same revision READY: rounds of blind reviews and revisions with a bounded stop rule. - **lls-ai-data-analysis-starter**: 带普通用户把 Excel、CSV 或表格问题转成可复核的数据分析。用于第一次做数据清洗、描述统计、分组比较、趋势、异常检查、图表和结论汇报时；必须先明确业务问题、字段含义、时间范围与统计口径，再检查数据质量并生成可重复步骤，不把相关性写成因果，也不根据缺失数据编造结论。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-ai-data-analysis-starter) · ★5)
- **lls-ai-drawing-prompt-coach**: 把用户的模糊画面想法整理成可测试、可迭代的文生图提示词和视觉方案。用于角色、场景、商品氛围图、插画、封面素材或概念图创作时；必须先明确画面用途、主体、叙事瞬间、构图、光线、色彩与限制，一次只调整一个变量并记录结果，不堆砌互相冲突的风格词。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-ai-drawing-prompt-coach) · ★5)
- **lls-ai-microdrama-planner**: 把一个题材想法发展成可连续生产的 AI 漫剧或短剧方案。用于确定受众承诺、人物欲望、冲突引擎、单集节拍、悬念钩子、分镜资产与连续性规则时；必须区分故事设定、已发生事实和待验证假设，不用随机反转、旁白堆积或视觉奇观代替因果推进。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-ai-microdrama-planner) · ★5)
- **lls-ai-novel-prompt-engineer**: 为中长篇小说建立可持续迭代的提示词与状态管理系统。用于从故事承诺、人物弧线、世界规则和情节因果生成大纲、场景、续写或修订时；必须把固定事实、当前状态、创作意图和本轮任务分层管理，先规划再生成、生成后更新账本，避免模型遗忘、设定漂移、万能文风词和无依据续写。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-ai-novel-prompt-engineer) · ★5)
- **lls-ai-poster-design-coach**: 把活动、课程、产品或品牌信息整理成可执行的海报内容与版式方案。用于需要明确传播目标、信息优先级、文案、尺寸、安全区、视觉方向、生成素材和导出验收时；必须先建立信息层级，再设计版式和图像，不把全部文字塞进画面，也不依赖图像模型生成准确正文。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-ai-poster-design-coach) · ★5)
- **lls-ai-video-script-planner**: 把主题、受众、素材和制作条件规划成可拍摄或可由视频生成工具执行的分镜脚本。用于知识视频、产品演示、品牌故事、教程或 AI 辅助视频制作，需要故事节拍、旁白、镜头、画面提示、素材清单和制作验收时；必须先锁定传播目标与真实证据，再设计镜头，不用华丽画面掩盖内容缺口。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-ai-video-script-planner) · ★5)
- **lls-aida-marketing-copywriter**: 用 AIDA 结构把真实产品、课程、活动或服务材料写成可验证的营销文案。用于广告、落地页、私域消息、海报文案和短视频转化段落时；必须先明确受众处境、产品事实、可信证据、渠道和行动成本，再组织注意、兴趣、渴望与行动，不制造虚假稀缺、伪造评价、夸张收益或隐藏重要限制。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-aida-marketing-copywriter) · ★5)
- **lls-article-screenshot**: 为教程、产品复盘和操作说明规划并采集能证明关键步骤的真实界面截图。用于决定截图证据点、清理演示环境、在 macOS 选择区域/窗口/全屏模式、规范命名、生成 Markdown 引用和交付前隐私复核时；必须先写每张图要证明的事实，优先最小范围截图，不用装饰性图片冒充执行证据。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-article-screenshot) · ★5)
- **lls-best-minds-consultation**: 为高不确定决策选择少量相关专家视角，分别论证后汇总共识、冲突、行动和反证条件。 当用户面对相应复杂任务并需要可验证结果时使用。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/adapted/lls-best-minds-consultation) · ★5)
- **lls-best-practices-researcher**: 把“大家都这么做”改造成一份有来源等级、有适用条件、能落地验证的研究结论。 当用户需要把相关任务变成有证据、可验证的交付时使用。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-best-practices-researcher) · ★5)
- **lls-chinese-humor**: 把已经明确的中文课程、口播、文章或现场表达改得更轻松、更有记忆点。用于根据受众、关系、场合和原意选择反差、错位、回扣、自嘲、具体类比等幽默机制，并控制密度、落点和可朗读性时；必须先确定谁可以成为笑点对象，不拿创伤、疾病、弱势身份、客户失误和私人信息换笑声，也不用网络烂梗破坏可信度。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-chinese-humor) · ★5)
- **lls-code-anatomy**: 面向代码初学者和跨专业学习者的中文代码学习教练。用于用户说“解剖这段代码”“逐行讲代码”“看不懂这一行”“帮我做代码学习笔记”时；先建立数据流地图，再对不超过约 30 行的功能块做意图注释、最难行逐 token 解剖、安全小改动与运行验证，最后生成可复习的学习卡片、通用模式和 3 道自测题。不得只解释不练习，也不得把密钥、客户数据或生产配置写进笔记。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-code-anatomy) · ★5)
- **lls-deepseek-golden-rules**: 把复杂任务拆成可核验阶段，为速度、深度、工具和隐私选择合适的 DeepSeek 使用方式。 当用户面对相应复杂任务并需要可验证结果时使用。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-deepseek-golden-rules) · ★5)
